<h1 class="txt-color-red login-header-big">重要農產品每日價量資訊平台</h1>
<div class="hero">

    <div class="pull-left login-desc-box-l">
        <h4 class="paragraph-header">本平台整合農業部及所屬機關（構）公布之農、漁、畜產品之價格及交易量資訊，提供便捷之視覺化查詢功能，期協助本部暨所屬機關（構）同仁即時掌握農產品價量動向。</h4>
    </div>

        </p>
    </div>
    <div class="col-xs-12 col-sm-12 col-md-6 col-lg-6">
        <h5 class="about-heading">功能說明</h5>
        <p>
            本平台開發「近2週每日量價」、「每日量價趨勢」、「歷年每日量價走勢」、「歷年各月量價分布」等4項量價查詢功能，主要提供價格及交易量（毛豬另提供重量）整合資訊；功能下方列有歷年整合資訊及每日資料，後3項功能亦提供期間之查詢彈性，可依需調整圖表涵蓋時間，亦提供資料列印或下載等輸出服務。此外重要產銷事件簿提供使用者新增、刪除、編輯事件功能；監控資訊提供監控品項、期間、地點、價格及因應措施等資訊；輿情回應專區提供輿情發布及回應園地。
        </p>
    </div>

	<!-- Possible Classes

		* 'smart-style-{SKIN#}'
		* 'smart-rtl'         - Switch theme mode to RTL
		* 'menu-on-top'       - Switch to top navigation (no DOM change required)
		* 'no-menu'			  - Hides the menu completely
		* 'hidden-menu'       - Hides the main menu but still accessable by hovering over left edge
		* 'fixed-header'      - Fixes the header
		* 'fixed-navigation'  - Fixes the main menu
		* 'fixed-ribbon'      - Fixes breadcrumb
		* 'fixed-page-footer' - Fixes footer
		* 'container'         - boxed layout mode (non-responsive: will not work with fixed-navigation & fixed-ribbon)
	-->

<script>
	pageSetUp();

	pagefunction = function(){
        const pad = (v) => {
        return (v<10)?'0'+v:v
        };

        const getDateString = (d) => {
        let year = d.getFullYear().toString();
        let month = pad(d.getMonth()+1);
        let day = pad(d.getDate());
        let hour = pad(d.getHours());
        let min = pad(d.getMinutes());
        let sec = pad(d.getSeconds());
        //YYYYMMDDhhmmss
        return year+month+day;
        //YYYY-MM-DD hh:mm:ss
        //return year+"-"+month+"-"day+" "+hour+":"+min+":"+sec
        };
        const today = new Date();

        $('#festival-report-refresh-submit').on('click', function(e){
        var $container = $('#festival-report-container');
        var url = $container.attr('data-load-url');
        var sel_festival = document.getElementById("sel_festival");
        var festival_id = sel_festival.options[sel_festival.selectedIndex].value;
        var festival_name = sel_festival.options[sel_festival.selectedIndex].text;
        var sel_roc_year = document.getElementById("sel_roc_year");
        var roc_year = sel_roc_year.options[sel_roc_year.selectedIndex].value;

        data = {
        'festival_id': festival_id,
        'festival_name': festival_name,
        'refresh': 'True',
        'roc_year': roc_year,
        'oneday':'False',
        'item_search':'',
        'custom_search':'False',
        };

        loadURL(url, $container, data, "POST");
        });

        $("#download_oneday_table").on('click', function(e){
                $("#oneday_table").table2csv({
                filename:'oneday_'+getDateString(today)+'.csv',
                separator: ',',
                newline: '\n',
                quoteFields: true,
                excludeColumns: '',
                excludeRows: '',
                trimContent: true, // Trims the content of individual <th>, <td> tags of whitespaces. 
                }); 
        });

        $("#download_custom_search_table").on('click', function(e){
                $("#custom_search_table").table2csv({
                filename:'custom_search_'+getDateString(today)+'.csv',
                separator: ',',
                newline: '\n',
                quoteFields: true,
                excludeColumns: '',
                excludeRows: '',
                trimContent: true, // Trims the content of individual <th>, <td> tags of whitespaces. 
                }); 
        });

        $("#download_custom_search_table_volume").on('click', function(e){
                $("#custom_search_table_volume").table2csv({
                filename:'custom_search_volume_'+getDateString(today)+'.csv',
                separator: ',',
                newline: '\n',
                quoteFields: true,
                excludeColumns: '',
                excludeRows: '',
                trimContent: true, // Trims the content of individual <th>, <td> tags of whitespaces.

        $("#download_oneday_table_pdf").on('click', function(e){
                var doc = new jspdf.jsPDF();
                doc.addFileToVFS('TP-normal.ttf', font);
                doc.addFont('TP-normal.ttf', 'TP', 'normal');
                doc.setFont('TP');
                doc.autoTable({
                        html: '#oneday_table',
                        styles: { fillColor: [255, 255, 255], font: 'TP', fontStyle: 'normal', textColor: [0, 0, 0], halign: 'center' }
                });
                doc.save('oneday_'+getDateString(today)+'.pdf');
        });

        $("#download_custom_search_table_pdf").on('click', function(e){
                var doc = new jspdf.jsPDF('l','pt','a0');
                doc.addFileToVFS('TP-normal.ttf', font);
                doc.addFont('TP-normal.ttf', 'TP', 'normal');
                doc.setFont('TP');
                doc.setFontSize(12);
                doc.autoTable({
                        html: '#custom_search_table',
                        theme: 'striped', //striped(顏色交替),grid(框線),plain(無)
                        tableWidth: 'auto',//表格寬度,auto(自動縮小各欄位寬度至設定寬度中),wrap(超過部分會被裁切),具體數據
                        //startY: 0,//表格Y位置
                        margin: {top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0},//表格邊距
                        showHead: 'everyPage', //everyPage,firstPage,never
                        styles: { fillColor: [255, 255, 255], font: 'TP', fontSize: 4.5, fontStyle: 'normal', textColor: [0, 0, 0], halign: 'center' },
                        columnStyles: {
                                        0: {cellWidth: 22},
                                        1: {cellWidth: 70},
                                        }
                });
                doc.save('custom_search_A0_'+getDateString(today)+'.pdf');
        });

        $("#download_custom_search_table_pdf_volume").on('click', function(e){
                var doc = new jspdf.jsPDF('l','pt','a0');
                doc.addFileToVFS('TP-normal.ttf', font);
                doc.addFont('TP-normal.ttf', 'TP', 'normal');
                doc.setFont('TP');
                doc.setFontSize(12);
                doc.autoTable({
                        html: '#custom_search_table_volume',
                        theme: 'striped', //striped(顏色交替),grid(框線),plain(無)
                        tableWidth: 'auto',//表格寬度,auto(自動縮小各欄位寬度至設定寬度中),wrap(超過部分會被裁切),具體數據
                        //startY: 0,//表格Y位置
                        margin: {top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0},//表格邊距

                        styles: { fillColor: [255, 255, 255], font: 'TP', fontSize: 4.5, fontStyle: 'normal', textColor: [0, 0, 0], halign: 'center' },
                        columnStyles: {
                                        0: {cellWidth: 22},
                                        1: {cellWidth: 70},
                                        }
                });
                doc.save('custom_search_volume_A0_'+getDateString(today)+'.pdf');

          Parameter

Appendix

Alert Types

This section contains additional information on the types of alerts in the report.

  1. Cookie No HttpOnly Flag

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Cookie No HttpOnly Flag)
    CWE ID 1004
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://owasp.org/www-community/HttpOnly

  2. Cookie without SameSite Attribute

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Cookie without SameSite Attribute)
    CWE ID 1275
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-httpbis-cookie-same-site

  3. Cross-Domain JavaScript Source File Inclusion

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Cross-Domain JavaScript Source File Inclusion)
    CWE ID 829
    WASC ID 15

  4. Server Leaks Version Information via "Server" HTTP Response Header Field

    Source raised by a passive scanner (HTTP Server Response Header)
    CWE ID 497
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://httpd.apache.org/docs/current/mod/core.html#servertokens
    2. https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/previous-versions/msp-n-p/ff648552(v=pandp.10)
    3. https://www.troyhunt.com/shhh-dont-let-your-response-headers/

  5. X-Content-Type-Options Header Missing

    Source raised by a passive scanner (X-Content-Type-Options Header Missing)
    CWE ID 693
    WASC ID 15
    Reference
    1. https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/previous-versions/windows/internet-explorer/ie-developer/compatibility/gg622941(v=vs.85)
    2. https://owasp.org/www-community/Security_Headers

  6. ZAP is Out of Date

    Source raised by a passive scanner (ZAP is Out of Date)
    CWE ID 1104
    WASC ID 45
    Reference
    1. https://www.zaproxy.org/download/

  7. Authentication Request Identified

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Authentication Request Identified)
    Reference
    1. https://www.zaproxy.org/docs/desktop/addons/authentication-helper/auth-req-id/

  8. Information Disclosure - Information in Browser localStorage

    Source raised by a passive scanner (plugin ID: 120000)
    CWE ID 359
    WASC ID 13

  9. Information Disclosure - Suspicious Comments

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Information Disclosure - Suspicious Comments)
    CWE ID 615
    WASC ID 13

  10. Modern Web Application

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Modern Web Application)

  11. Session Management Response Identified

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Session Management Response Identified)
    Reference
    1. https://www.zaproxy.org/docs/desktop/addons/authentication-helper/session-mgmt-id/

  12. Tech Detected - Bootstrap

    Source raised by other tools/functionalities in ZAP (for example, fuzzer, HTTPS Info add-on, custom scripts...) (plugin ID: 10004)
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://getbootstrap.com

  13. Tech Detected - Django

    Source raised by other tools/functionalities in ZAP (for example, fuzzer, HTTPS Info add-on, custom scripts...) (plugin ID: 10004)
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://djangoproject.com

  14. Tech Detected - Font Awesome

    Source raised by other tools/functionalities in ZAP (for example, fuzzer, HTTPS Info add-on, custom scripts...) (plugin ID: 10004)
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://fontawesome.com/

  15. Tech Detected - Google Font API

    Source raised by other tools/functionalities in ZAP (for example, fuzzer, HTTPS Info add-on, custom scripts...) (plugin ID: 10004)
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://fonts.google.com/

  16. Tech Detected - Google Hosted Libraries

    Source raised by other tools/functionalities in ZAP (for example, fuzzer, HTTPS Info add-on, custom scripts...) (plugin ID: 10004)
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://developers.google.com/speed/libraries

  17. Tech Detected - cdnjs

    Source raised by other tools/functionalities in ZAP (for example, fuzzer, HTTPS Info add-on, custom scripts...) (plugin ID: 10004)
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://cdnjs.com

  18. Tech Detected - jQuery

    Source raised by other tools/functionalities in ZAP (for example, fuzzer, HTTPS Info add-on, custom scripts...) (plugin ID: 10004)
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://jquery.com

  19. Tech Detected - jQuery UI

    Source raised by other tools/functionalities in ZAP (for example, fuzzer, HTTPS Info add-on, custom scripts...) (plugin ID: 10004)
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://jqueryui.com

  20. Tech Detected - jsDelivr

    Source raised by other tools/functionalities in ZAP (for example, fuzzer, HTTPS Info add-on, custom scripts...) (plugin ID: 10004)
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://www.jsdelivr.com/

  21. User Controllable HTML Element Attribute (Potential XSS)

    Source raised by a passive scanner (User Controllable HTML Element Attribute (Potential XSS))
    CWE ID 20
    WASC ID 20
    Reference
    1. https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Input_Validation_Cheat_Sheet.html
